“That’s the jeep that reported Bish Ware going in on the bottom,” Mohandas Feinberg said. “I wonder if somebody inside mightn’t have gotten both the man on the jeep and Bish.”

“He could have left the jeep,” Joe said. “Maybe he went inside after Bish.”

“Funny he didn’t call in and say so,” somebody said.

“No, it isn’t,” I contradicted. “Manufacturers’ claims to the contrary, there is no such thing as a tap-proof radio. Maybe he wasn’t supposed to leave his post, but if he did, he used his head not advertising it.”
===Chinese===
「那正是报告比什·韦尔从底层进去的那辆吉普，」莫汉达斯·范伯格说。「我在想，会不会是里面的人把车上那人和比什一股脑儿都收拾了。」

「他兴许离开了吉普，」乔说。「没准是跟着比什进楼里去了。」

「怪就怪在他没呼一声报告，」有人说。

「不，一点也不怪，」我反驳道。「任凭厂商把牛皮吹上天，世上也没有窃听不了的电台这种东西。他多半本不该擅离岗位，可他既然离了，不声张才是动了脑子。」

===Original===
“That makes sense,” Oscar agreed. “Well, whatever happened, we’re not doing anything standing around up here. Let’s get it started.”

He walked away, raising his voice and calling, “*Pequod*! *Pequod*! *All hands on deck!*”

The others broke away from the group, shouting the names of their ships to rally their crews. I hurried over to the jeep and checked my equipment. There wasn’t too much film left in the big audiovisual, so I replaced it with a fresh sound-and-vision reel, good for another couple of hours, and then lifted to the ceiling. Worrying about Tom wouldn’t help Tom, and worrying about Bish wouldn’t help Bish, and I had a job to do.
===Chinese===
「这话在理，」奥斯卡附和道。「这样吧，不管出了什么事，咱们在这儿干站着是干不出名堂的。动手吧。」

他走开去，扯开嗓子喊道：「裴廓德号（*Pequod*）！裴廓德号！*全体上甲板*（All hands on deck）！」

众人纷纷散开，高声喊着各自船名召集船员。我赶紧跑到吉普旁检查装备。那台大声像机（audiovisual）里的胶带所剩不多，我便换上一卷新的声像带，够再拍两个来钟头，然后升到顶棚高度。替汤姆操心帮不了汤姆，替比什操心也帮不了比什，而我手头有活儿要干。

===Original===
What I was getting now, and I was glad I was starting a fresh reel for it, was the beginning of the First Fenris Civil War. A long time from now, when Fenris was an important planet in the Federation, maybe they’d make today a holiday, like Bastille Day or the Fourth of July or Federation Day. Maybe historians, a couple of centuries from now, would call me an important primary source, and if Cesário’s religion was right, maybe I’d be one of them, saying, “Well, after all, is Boyd such a reliable source? He was only seventeen years old at the time.”
===Chinese===
这会儿我拍下的，可就是芬尼斯第一次内战（First Fenris Civil War）的开场了——难怪我庆幸为它换上了卷新带。多年以后，等芬尼斯成了联邦里的重要行星，没准他们会把今天定为节日，好比巴士底日（Bastille Day）、七月四日独立日（Fourth of July）或者联邦日（Federation Day）。再过几百年，没准历史学家会把我称作重要的原始史料；而要是塞萨里奥信的那门宗教靠谱，说不定我自己也会跻身他们中间，说上一句：「唉，说到底，博伊德（Boyd）这史料可靠吗？他当年不过十七岁嘛。」

===Original===
Finally, after a lot of yelling and confusion, the Rebel Army got moving. We all went up to Main City Level and went down Broadway, spreading out side streets when we began running into the cordon that had been thrown around Hunters’ Hall. They were mostly men from the waterfront who hadn’t gotten to the wax fire, and they must have stripped the guns off half the ships in the harbor and mounted them on lorries or cargo skids.

Nobody, not even Joe Kivelson, wanted to begin with any massed frontal attack on Hunters’ Hall.

“We’ll have to bombard the place,” he was saying. “We try to rush it and we’ll lose half our gang before we get in. One man with good cover and a machine gun’s good for a couple of hundred in the open.”
===Chinese===
终于，在一番叫嚷与混乱之后，起义军（Rebel Army）开拔了。我们全都升上主城层，沿百老汇（Broadway）大街一路推进，一碰上围住猎手会堂的封锁线，便散入两侧横街。他们大多是没赶上那场蜡火的滨水区汉子，准是把港里半数船上的炮都拆了下来，装上卡车或货运载板。

谁都不想一上来就对猎手会堂发动什么集群正面强攻——连乔·基维尔森也不例外。

「咱们得炮轰那地方，」他正这么说。「硬要强冲，人还没进楼，咱们就得折损一半。一个占着好掩体的机枪手，收拾旷地上的二百来号人绰绰有余。」

===Original===
“Bish may be inside,” I mentioned.

“Yes,” Oscar said, “and even aside from that, that building was built with our money. Let’s don’t burn the house down to get rid of the cockroaches.”

“Well, how are you going to do it, then?” Joe wanted to know. Rule out frontal attack and Joe’s at the end of his tactics.
===Chinese===
「比什可能在里面，」我提了一句。

「是啊，」奥斯卡说，「就算撇开这一层，那座楼也是拿咱们的钱盖起来的。总不能为了灭蟑螂，把房子烧塌吧。」

「那你说，打算怎么个干法？」乔想要知道。排除了正面强攻，乔的战术也就见了底。

===Original===
“You stay up here. Keep them amused with a little smallarms fire at the windows and so on. I’ll take about a dozen men and go down to Second Level. If we can’t do anything else, we can bring a couple of skins of tallow-wax down and set fire to it and smoke them out.”

That sounded like a pretty expensive sort of smudge, but seeing how much wax Ravick had burned uptown, it was only fair to let him in on some of the smoke. I mentioned that if we got into the building and up to Main City Level, we’d need some way of signaling to avoid being shot by our own gang, and got the wavelength combination of the Pequod scout boat, which Joe and Oscar were using for a command car. Oscar picked ten or twelve men, and they got into a lorry and went uptown and down a vehicle shaft to Second Level. I followed in my jeep, even after Oscar and his crowd let down and got out, and hovered behind them as they advanced on foot to Hunters’ Hall.
===Chinese===
「你们留在上头。朝窗户什么的用轻武器零零星星打点枪，吊住他们的兴致。我带上十来个人，到下第二层去。实在没别的办法，咱们就抬几皮囊脂蜡下去点着，把他们熏出来。」

这么个熏烟法子可真够昂贵的，不过想想拉维克在上城烧掉了多少蜡，让他也分着抽上几口烟，才算公平。我提出，若是我们进了楼、再上到主城层，得有个发信号的法子，免得挨了自家人的枪，于是问来了裴廓德号侦察艇（scout boat）的波长组合——乔和奥斯卡正拿它当指挥车用。奥斯卡挑了十来个人，上了一辆卡车，开往上城，顺一条车辆竖井（vehicle shaft）降到下第二层。我驾着吉普一路跟随；等奥斯卡那伙人降落出车之后，我仍悬停在他们身后，随着他们朝猎手会堂步行推进。

===Original===
The Second Level Down was the vehicle storage, where the derricks and other equipment had been kept. It was empty now except for a workbench, a hand forge and some other things like that, a few drums of lubricant, and several piles of sheet metal. Oscar and his men got inside and I followed, going up to the ceiling. I was the one who saw the man lying back of a pile of sheet metal, and called their attention.

He wore boat-clothes and had black whiskers, and he had a knife and a pistol on his belt. At first I thought he was dead. A couple of Oscar’s followers, dragging him out, said:

“He’s been sleep-gassed.”

Somebody else recognized him. He was the lone man who had been on guard in the jeep. The jeep was nowhere in sight.
===Chinese===
下第二层是车辆存放区，吊车和其他装备原先都搁在这儿。如今空空荡荡，只剩一张工作台、一座手锻炉之类的几样东西、几桶润滑油和几堆薄钢板。奥斯卡和他的人进去了，我跟在后面，升到顶棚。那堆薄钢板后面躺着一个人，是我先看见的，出声唤起大家的注意。

他一身船员装束，络腮胡黑黑的，腰带上挂着刀和手枪。起初我以为他已经死了。奥斯卡手下有两个人把他拖出来，说：

「他是中了催眠气体。」

另有人认出了他。他就是先前独自守在那辆吉普上的人。那辆吉普却哪儿也看不见了。

===Original===
I began to be really worried. My lighter gadget could have been what had gassed him. It probably was; there weren’t many sleep-gas weapons on Fenris. I had to get fills made up specially for mine. So it looked to me as though somebody had gotten mine off Bish, and then used it to knock out our guard. Taken it off his body I guessed. That crowd wasn’t any more interested in taking prisoners alive than we were.

We laid the man on a workbench and put a rolled-up sack under his head for a pillow. Then we started up the enclosed stairway. I didn’t think we were going to run into any trouble, though I kept my hand close to my gun. If they’d knocked out the guard, they had a way out, and none of them wanted to stay in that building any longer than they had to.
===Chinese===
我开始真正担心起来。放倒他的，多半就是我那件打火机武器（lighter gadget）。十有八九是它；芬尼斯上催眠气体武器没有几件，我那件的补充气剂还得专门配制。照这么看，是有人把我的那件从比什身上缴了去，又拿它撂倒了咱们的哨兵。我猜是从他尸身上取下来的。那伙人对抓活口的兴趣，也跟咱们彼此彼此。

我们把那人抬上一张工作台，脑袋底下垫了卷起的麻袋当枕头。然后我们顺着封闭楼梯往上走。我不觉得会撞上什么麻烦，不过手始终不离腰间的枪。他们既然放倒了哨兵，那就是有了脱身之路，他们谁也不想在那座楼里多待一刻。

===Original===
The First Level Down was mostly storerooms, with nobody in any of them. As we went up the stairway to the Main City Level, we could hear firing outside. Nobody inside was shooting back. I unhooked my handphone.

“We’re in,” I said when Joe Kivelson answered. “Stop the shooting; we’re coming up to the vehicle port.”

“Might as well. Nobody’s paying any attention to it,” he said.

The firing slacked off as the word was passed around the perimeter, and finally it stopped entirely. We went up into the open arched vehicle port. It was barricaded all around, and there were half a dozen machine guns set up, but not a living thing.
===Chinese===
下第一层大多是库房，间间空无一人。我们顺楼梯朝主城层上去时，听得见外面的枪声。楼里没有人还击。我摘下手持话机。

「我们进来了，」乔·基维尔森应答时我说。「停止射击；我们这就上到车辆门廊（vehicle port）。」

「停就停吧。反正这枪也没人理会，」他说。

命令沿封锁线一路传开，枪声渐渐稀落，最后彻底停了。我们上到敞开的拱形车辆门廊。四周垒起了街垒，架着六挺机枪，却不见一个活物。

===Original===
“We’re going up,” I said. “They’ve all lammed out. The place is empty.”

“You don’t know that,” Oscar chided. “It might be bulging with Ravick’s thugs, waiting for us to come walking up and be mowed down.”

*Possible. Highly improbable, though*, I thought. The escalators weren’t running, and we weren’t going to alert any hypothetical ambush by starting them. We tiptoed up, and I even drew my pistol to show that I wasn’t being foolhardy. The big social room was empty. A couple of us went over and looked behind the bar, which was the only hiding place in it. Then we went back to the rear and tiptoed to the third floor.
===Chinese===
「我们上去啦，」我说。「他们全溜了。楼里空了。」

「你可说不准，」奥斯卡责备道。「说不定里头挤满了拉维克的打手，正等着咱们走上去，好一锅端呢。」

*有可能。不过极不可能*，我想。自动扶梯停着没开，我们也不打算去启动它们、惊动那场子虚乌有的伏击。我们蹑手蹑脚往上走，我甚至还拔出了手枪，以示自己并非有勇无谋。大交谊厅空空如也。我们当中有几个人走过去，查看了吧台后面——那是厅里唯一的藏身之处。然后我们折向后部，蹑手蹑脚上了三楼。

===Original===
The meeting room was empty. So were the offices behind it. I looked in all of them, expecting to find Bish Ware’s body. Maybe a couple of other bodies, too. I’d seen him shoot the tread-snail, and I didn’t think he’d die unpaid for. In Steve Ravick’s office, the safe was open and a lot of papers had been thrown out. I pointed that out to Oscar, and he nodded. After seeing that, he seemed to relax, as though he wasn’t expecting to find anybody any more. We went to the third floor. Ravick’s living quarters were there, and they were magnificently luxurious. The hunters, whose money had paid for all that magnificence and luxury, cursed.

There were no bodies there, either, or on the landing stage above. I unhooked the radio again.
===Chinese===
会议室空无一人，后面的一间间办公室也全都空着。我逐间看过去，一心指望着发现比什·韦尔的尸体。没准还有另外几具。我见过他射杀履带蜗牛（tread-snail）的身手，不信他会白白送死、不拉个垫背的。在史蒂夫·拉维克的办公室里，保险柜敞着口，大批文件被翻扔了出来。我把这指给奥斯卡看，他点了点头。看到这个，他似乎松了一口气，仿佛再也不指望找到任何人了。我们上了三楼。拉维克的住所就在那一层，陈设之豪华，堂皇夺目。猎手们——正是他们的钱付起了这一份堂皇与豪华——破口大骂。

那一层也没有尸体，上面的降落台（landing stage）上同样没有。我又摘下话机。

===Original===
“You can come in, now,” I said. “The place is empty. Nobody here but us Vigilantes.”

“Huh?” Joe couldn’t believe that. “How’d they get out?”

“They got out on the Second Level Down.” I told him about the sleep-gassed guard.
===Chinese===
「现在可以进来了，」我说。「楼里空了。除了咱们自警队（Vigilantes），一个人影也没有。」

「啊？」乔不敢相信。「他们是怎么出去的？」

「他们是从下第二层出去的。」我把哨兵中了催眠气体的事跟他说了。

===Original===
“Did you bring him to? What did he say?”

“Nothing; we didn’t. We can’t. You get sleep-gassed, you sleep till you wake up. That ought to be two to four hours for this fellow.”

“Well, hold everything; we’re coming in.”
===Chinese===
「你们弄醒他没有？他说了些什么？」

「什么也没说；我们没弄醒他。也弄不醒。挨了催眠气体，就只能一觉睡到自然醒。这一位，够呛要睡上两个到四个钟头。」

「这样啊，都先别动；我们进来了。」

===Original===
We were all in the social room; a couple of the men had poured drinks or drawn themselves beers at the bar and rung up no sale on the cash register. Somebody else had a box of cigars he’d picked up in Ravick’s quarters on the fourth floor and was passing them around. Joe and about two or three hundred other hunters came crowding up the escalator, which they had turned on below.

“You didn’t find Bish Ware, either, I’ll bet,” Joe was saying.

“I’m afraid they took him along for a hostage,” Oscar said. “The guard was knocked out with Walt’s gas gadget, that Bish was carrying.”
===Chinese===
我们全都聚在大交谊厅里；有几个汉子在吧台给自己斟了酒、接了啤酒，在收银机上按的是「无销售」键了事。另有一个人捧着一盒雪茄四处散发——那是他从四楼拉维克的套房里顺来的。乔和大约两三百名猎手挤上了自动扶梯，他们在下面把扶梯开动了。

「我敢打赌，你们也没找见比什·韦尔，」乔正说着。

「恐怕是他们把他掳走当人质了，」奥斯卡说。「哨兵是被沃尔特那件喷气玩意儿撂倒的——比什身上正带着它。」

===Original===
“Ha!” Joe cried. “Bet you it was the other way round; Bish took them out.”

That started an argument. While it was going on, I went to the communication screen and got the *Times*, and told Dad what had happened.

“Yes,” he said. “That was what I was afraid you’d find. Glenn Murell called in from the spaceport a few minutes ago. He says Mort Hallstock came in with his car, and he heard from some of the workmen that Bish Ware, Steve Ravick and Leo Belsher came in on the Main City Level in a jeep. They claimed protection from a mob, and Captain Courtland’s police are protecting them.”
===Chinese===
「哈！」乔嚷道。「我跟你赌，事情正相反；是比什把他们带出去的。」

这话激起一场争论。趁他们争得热闹，我走到通信屏幕前接通《时报》，把事情经过告诉了爸爸。

「是啊，」他说。「我就怕你们查出这么个结果。几分钟前，格伦·穆雷尔（Glenn Murell）从太空港来了电话。他说莫特·霍尔斯特克（Mort Hallstock）乘车进了太空港，他还从一些工人那儿听说，比什·韦尔、史蒂夫·拉维克和利奥·贝尔歇（Leo Belsher）是乘一辆吉普、打主城层进的太空港。他们声称受到暴徒威胁要求庇护，眼下科特兰船长（Captain Courtland）手下的警察正在保护他们。」

## Vigilantes / 自警队

===Original===
When the *Pequod* surfaced under the city roof, I saw what was cooking. There were twenty or more ships, either on the concrete docks or afloat in the pools. The waterfront was crowded with men in boat clothes, forming little knots and breaking up to join other groups, all milling about talking excitedly. Most of them were armed; not just knives and pistols, which is normal costume, but heavy rifles or submachine guns. Down to the left, there was a commotion and people were getting out of the way as a dozen men come pushing through, towing a contragravity skid with a 50 mm ship’s gun on it. I began not liking the looks of things, and Glenn Murell, who had come up from his nap below, was liking it even less. He’d come to Fenris to buy tallow-wax, not to fight a civil war. I didn’t want any of that stuff, either. Getting rid of Ravick, Hallstock and Belsher would come under the head of civic improvements, but towns are rarely improved by having battles fought in them.

===Chinese===
裴廓德号（*Pequod*）在城市穹顶下浮出水面时，我就看出这事酝酿着什么名堂了。港里停着二十来艘船，有的上了混凝土船坞，有的浮泊在水湾里。滨水区挤满了身穿船上装束的汉子，一小堆一小堆地聚着，散开又汇进别堆，转来转去，个个谈得激奋。他们多半带着家伙；不光是刀子和手枪——那本是寻常装束——还有重型步枪、冲锋枪。左下方一阵骚动，人们纷纷闪开，十来条汉子推推搡搡挤过人群，拖着一具载着 50 毫米舰炮的反重力载板。我开始不喜欢眼前的阵仗，而从下面舱里睡罢午觉上来的格伦·穆雷尔（Glenn Murell），比我更不喜欢。他是来芬尼斯买脂蜡的，不是来打内战的。这种热闹我也不想沾。除掉拉维克、霍尔斯特克和贝尔歇，固然归得上市政改良一类，可城市很少因为有人在城里开仗而变得更好。

===Original===
*Maybe I should have played dumb and waited till I’d talked to Dad face to face, before making any statements about what had happened on the *Javelin**, I thought. Then I shrugged that off. From the minute the *Javelin* had failed to respond to Dad’s screen-call and the general call had gone out to the hunter-fleet, everybody had been positive of what had happened. It was too much like the loss of the *Claymore*, which had made Ravick president of the Coop.

===Chinese===
*兴许我该装聋作哑，等同爸爸当面谈过、再就标枪号（*Javelin*）上出的事发表什么说法*，我心想。随即又耸耸肩，把这念头甩开了。打标枪号没应答爸爸的屏幕呼叫、向全体猎怪船发出总呼的那一刻起，人人都已断定出了什么事。这情形跟阔剑号（*Claymore*）的失事实在太像了——正是那一遭，让拉维克当上了合作社主席。

===Original===
Port Sandor had just gotten all of Steve Ravick that anybody could take. They weren’t going to have any more of him, and that was all there was to it.

===Chinese===
对史蒂夫·拉维克（Steve Ravick），桑多尔港已经受够了能受的极限。人们再不想多忍他一分，事情就这么定了。

===Original===
Joe Kivelson was grumbling about his broken arm; that meant that when a fight started, he could only go in swinging with one fist, and that would cut the fun in half. Another reason why Joe is a wretched shot is that he doesn’t like pistols. They’re a little too impersonal to suit him. They weren’t for Oscar Fujisawa; he had gotten a Mars-Consolidated Police Special out of the chart-table drawer and put it on, and he was loading cartridges into a couple of spare clips. Down on the main deck, the gunner was serving out small arms, and there was an acrimonious argument because everybody wanted a chopper and there weren’t enough choppers to go around. Oscar went over to the ladder head and shouted down at them.

“Knock off the argument, down there; you people are all going to stay on the ship. I’m going up to the *Times*; as soon as I’m off, float her out into the inner channel and keep her afloat, and don’t let anybody aboard you’re not sure of.”

===Chinese===
乔·基维尔森（Joe Kivelson）正为断了的那条胳膊发牢骚；那就是说，真打起来，他只能挥着一只拳头往上冲，乐趣得减掉一半。乔枪法稀烂还有个缘故：他不喜欢手枪。这玩意儿太不讲人情，不合他的脾胃。奥斯卡·藤泽（Oscar Fujisawa）却不然；他从海图桌抽屉里取出一支火星联合「警用特制型」（Mars-Consolidated Police Special）挎上，正往几只备用弹匣里压子弹。下面主甲板上，炮手正在发放轻武器，那边吵得不可开交——人人都要冲锋枪，冲锋枪却不够分的。奥斯卡走到梯口，朝下面喊话。

「别吵了，下面；你们这些人统统留在船上。我要上《时报》（*Times*）去；我一离船，就把船浮出去停进内航道，保持浮泊，拿不准的人，谁也不许放上来。」

===Original===
“That where we’re going?” Joe Kivelson asked.

“Sure. That’s the safest place in town for Mr. Murell and I want to find out exactly what’s going on here.”

“Well, here; you don’t need to put me in storage,” Murell protested. “I can take care of myself.”

===Chinese===
「我们要去的就是那儿？」乔·基维尔森问。

「正是。对穆雷尔先生来说，那是全城最安全的地方，再说我也想弄清楚这儿到底是怎么回事。」

「哎，得了；你们用不着把我当库存收着，」穆雷尔抗议道。「我照顾得了自己。」

===Original===
*Add, Famous Last Words*, I thought.

“I’m sure of it, but we can’t take any chances,” Oscar told him. “Right now, you are Fenris’s Indispensable Man. If you’re not around to buy tallow-wax, Ravick’s won the war.”

===Chinese===
*补一句：典型的「名言遗言」*，我心想。

「这一点我信，可我们不能冒半点风险，」奥斯卡对他说。「眼下的您，是芬尼斯『不可或缺之人』。您要是不在了、没人收购脂蜡，拉维克就赢了这场仗。」

===Original===
Oscar and Murell and Joe and Tom Kivelson and I went down into the boat; somebody opened the port and we floated out and lifted onto the Second Level Down. There was a fringe of bars and cafés and dance halls and outfitters and ship chandlers for a couple of blocks back, and then we ran into the warehouse district. Oscar ran up town to a vehicle shaft above the *Times* Building, careful to avoid the neighborhood of Hunters’ Hall or the Municipal Building.

===Chinese===
奥斯卡、穆雷尔、乔、汤姆·基维尔森（Tom Kivelson）和我下到小艇里；有人开了艇门，我们浮出去，升上下第二层（Second Level Down）。往里两三个街区，酒吧、咖啡馆、舞厅、装备行和船具店连成一道边儿，再往里便是仓库区。奥斯卡把艇开往上城《时报》大楼上方的一个车辆竖井，一路留神避开猎手会堂（Hunters' Hall）和市政大楼（Municipal Building）一带。

===Original===
There was a big crowd around the *Times*, mostly business district people and quite a few women. They were mostly out on the street and inside the street-floor vehicle port. Not a disorderly crowd, but I noticed quite a few rifles and submachine guns. As we slipped into the vehicle port, they recognized the *Pequod*’s boat, and there was a rush after it. We had trouble getting down without setting it on anybody, and more trouble getting out of it. They were all friendly—too friendly for comfort. They began cheering us as soon as they saw us.

===Chinese===
《时报》四周聚着一大群人，多半是商业区的市民，还有不少女人。他们大都站在街上，或者挤在临街层的车辆门廊里。秩序倒不见乱，但我注意到不少步枪和冲锋枪。我们的艇溜进车辆门廊时，他们认出了裴廓德号的小艇，人群顿时涌了上来。艇往下落时既不能压着人，费了好大劲；下艇更是费劲。人人都亲热得很——亲热得让人发怵。他们一瞧见我们就欢呼起来。

===Original===
Oscar got Joe Kivelson, with his arm in a sling, out in front where he could be seen, and began shouting: “Please make way; this man’s been injured. Please don’t crowd; we have an injured man here.” The crowd began shoving back, and in the rear I could hear them taking it up: “Joe Kivelson; he’s been hurt. They’re carrying Joe Kivelson off.” That made Joe curse a blue streak, and somebody said, “Oh, he’s been hurt real bad; just listen to him!”

===Chinese===
奥斯卡把吊着绷带的乔·基维尔森推到前排显眼处，开始高声喊道：「请让让路；这人受了伤。请别围拢；我们这儿有伤员。」人群往后退去，我听见后排把这话接力传开：「乔·基维尔森；他受了伤。他们正把乔·基维尔森抬走呢。」这话惹得乔破口大骂了一长串，有人便接话：「哎呀，他伤得可不轻；听听他那嗓门！」

===Original===
When we got up to the editorial floor, Dad and Bish Ware and a few others were waiting at the elevator for us. Bish was dressed as he always was, in his conservative black suit, with the organic opal glowing in his neckcloth. Dad had put a coat on over his gun. Julio was wearing two pistols and a knife a foot long. There was a big crowd in the editorial office—ships’ officers, merchants, professional people. I noticed Sigurd Ngozori, the banker, and Professor Hartzenbosch—he was wearing a pistol, too, rather self-consciously—and the Zen Buddhist priest, who evidently had something under his kimono. They all greeted us enthusiastically and shook hands with us. I noticed that Joe Kivelson was something less than comfortable about shaking hands with Bish Ware. The fact that Bish had started the search for the *Javelin* that had saved our lives didn’t alter the opinion Joe had formed long ago that Bish was just a worthless old souse. Joe’s opinions are all collapsium-plated and impervious to outside influence.

===Chinese===
我们上到编辑层时，爸爸、比什·韦尔（Bish Ware）和另外几位正在电梯口等我们。比什还是一身老打扮，那套古板的黑西装，领结上的有机欧泊（organic opal）莹莹放光。爸爸在枪外头罩了件外衣。胡利奥（Julio）挎着两把手枪，还带着一柄一英尺长的刀。编辑室里挤着一大群人——各船的船长船副、商贾、专业人士。我瞧见了银行家西格德·恩戈佐里（Sigurd Ngozori）和哈岑博施教授（Professor Hartzenbosch）——他也带着枪，神色颇不自在——还有那位禅宗法师（Zen Buddhist priest），和服（kimono）底下显然也藏着家伙。他们全都热情地招呼我们、同我们握手。我注意到，乔·基维尔森同比什·韦尔握手时，多少有些不自在。比什发起对标枪号的搜寻、救了我们众人的性命，这一事实也改变不了乔多年前就定下的看法：比什不过是个不中用的老酒鬼。乔的那些见解，全都镀着坍缩素装甲，外界影响一丝一毫也渗不进去。

===Original===
I got Bish off to one side as we were going into the editorial room.

“How did you get onto it?” I asked.

===Chinese===
进编辑室的时候，我把比什拉到一边。

「你是怎么察觉出来的？」我问。

===Original===
He chuckled deprecatingly. “No trick at all,” he said. “I just circulated and bought drinks for people. The trouble with Ravick’s gang, it’s an army of mercenaries. They’ll do anything for the price of a drink, and as long as my rich uncle stays solvent, I always have the price of a drink. In the five years I’ve spent in this Garden Spot of the Galaxy, I’ve learned some pretty surprising things about Steve Ravick’s operations.”

===Chinese===
他呵呵一笑，带着几分自贬。「毫无诀窍，」他说。「我不过是四处转悠，给人买酒喝。拉维克那帮人的毛病在于，那是一支雇佣军。为一杯酒的价钱，他们什么都肯干；而只要我那位阔叔叔不破产，一杯酒的价钱我总是出得起的。在这处银河系头号花园胜地待的五年里，我对史蒂夫·拉维克的种种营生，摸到了几桩相当叫人吃惊的内情。」

===Original===
“Well, surely, nobody was going around places like Martian Joe’s or One Eye Swanson’s boasting that they’d put a time bomb aboard the *Javelin*,” I said.

“It came to pretty nearly that,” Bish said. “You’d be amazed at how careless people who’ve had their own way for a long time can get. For instance, I’ve known for some time that Ravick has spies among the crews of a lot of hunter-ships. I tried, a few times, to warn some of these captains, but except for Oscar Fujisawa and Corkscrew Finnegan, none of them would listen to me. It wasn’t that they had any doubt that Ravick would do that; they just wouldn’t believe that any of their crew were traitors.

===Chinese===
「可总不见得有人会跑到『火星乔』酒吧（Martian Joe's）或者『独眼斯旺森』（One Eye Swanson's）那种地方，吹嘘自己在标枪号上放了定时炸弹吧，」我说。

「也差不离了，」比什说。「一贯只手遮天惯了的人能马虎到什么地步，你准会大吃一惊。比方说，我早就知道，拉维克在不少猎怪船的船员里安着眼线。我试着提醒过其中几位船长，可除了奥斯卡·藤泽和『螺丝锥』芬尼根（Corkscrew Finnegan），没一个肯听我的。倒不是他们怀疑拉维克干不出这种事；他们只是不肯相信自己的船员里有内奸。

===Original===
“I’ve suspected this Devis for a long time, and I’ve spoken to Ramón Llewellyn about him, but he just let it go in one ear and out the other. For one thing, Devis always has more money to spend than his share of the *Javelin* take would justify. He’s the showoff type; always buying drinks for everybody and playing the big shot. Claims to win it gambling, but all the times I’ve ever seen him gambling, he’s been losing.

“I knew about this hoard of wax we saw the day Murell came in for some time. I always thought it was being held out to squeeze a better price out of Belsher and Ravick. Then this friend of mine with whom I was talking aboard the *Peenemünde* mentioned that Murell seemed to know more about the tallow-wax business than about literary matters, and after what happened at the meeting and afterward, I began putting two and two together. When I crashed that party at Hunters’ Hall, I heard a few things, and they all added up.

===Chinese===
「这个德维斯，我疑心好久了，也跟拉蒙·卢埃林（Ramón Llewellyn）提过他，可他只当耳边风，左耳进右耳出。单说一条：德维斯手头花销的钱，总比他按标枪号的分成该挣的那份多。他是那种好显摆的角色；见人就请酒，充大亨。嘴上说是赌钱赢的，可我每回见他上赌桌，回回都在输。

「穆雷尔进港那天我们看到的那囤蜡，我晓得它的底细有些日子了。我一直当那是扣着不卖，好从贝尔歇和拉维克手里多榨些价钱。后来，我在佩内明德号（*Peenemünde*）上同一位朋友聊天，他提起穆雷尔对脂蜡行当的了解，似乎比对本行文学还深；再加上会场里和会后出的那些事，我便开始把零碎线索往一处串。等我闯进猎手会堂那场聚会，又听到了些风声，条条都对上了榫。

===Original===
“And then, about thirty hours after the Javelin left port, I was in the Happy Haven, and who should I see, buying drinks for the house, but Al Devis. I let him buy me one, and he told me he’d strained his back hand-lifting a power-unit cartridge. A square dance got started a little later, and he got into it. His back didn’t look very strained to me. And then I heard a couple of characters in One Eye Swanson’s betting that the *Javelin* would never make port again.”

===Chinese===
「再后来，标枪号出港大约三十个钟头，我正在『安乐窝』酒吧（Happy Haven），你道我看见谁在请全场的客？阿尔·德维斯（Al Devis）。我领了他一杯，他告诉我，徒手搬动力单元弹，把腰给闪了。过了一阵，有人跳起方块舞，他也下了场。依我看，他那腰可不像闪了的样子。再后来，我又听见『独眼斯旺森』里两个角色打赌，说标枪号再也回不了港。」

===Original===
I knew what had happened from then on. If it hadn’t been for Bish Ware, we’d still be squatting around a fire down on the coast of Hermann Reuch’s Land till it got too cold to cut wood, and then we’d freeze. I mentioned that, but Bish just shrugged it off and suggested we go on in and see what was happening inside.

===Chinese===
那以后的事我就都知道了。要不是比什·韦尔，这会儿我们还在赫尔曼·罗伊希之地（Hermann Reuch's Land）的海岸边围着火堆蹲着，挨到天冷得砍不动柴，然后活活冻僵。这话我提了，可比什只耸耸肩带过，提议我们进去瞧瞧里头正有什么动静。

===Original===
“Where is Al Devis?” I asked. “A lot of people want to talk to him.”

“I know they do. I want to get to him first, while he’s still in condition to do some talking of his own. But he just dropped out of sight, about the time your father started calling the *Javelin*.”

===Chinese===
「阿尔·德维斯人在哪儿？」我问。「好多人有话要跟他谈。」

「我知道他们想。我还想赶在他们前头找到他，趁他还亲口说得上话。可他偏偏就在你父亲开始呼叫标枪号那阵子，人不见了踪影。」

===Original===
“Ah!” I drew a finger across under my chin, and mentioned the class of people who tell no tales. Bish shook his head slowly.

“I doubt it,” he said. “Not unless it was absolutely necessary. That sort of thing would have a discouraging effect the next time Ravick wanted a special job done. I’m pretty sure he isn’t at Hunters’ Hall, but he’s hiding somewhere.”

===Chinese===
「啊！」我用手指在下巴底下横着一划，提了一句「死无对证」的那一类人。比什缓缓摇头。

「我看不至于，」他说。「除非万不得已。那种事一做，下回拉维克再想办什么特别的差事，人家就得掂量着不敢应了。我敢肯定他不在猎手会堂，不过正躲在什么去处。」

===Original===
Joe Kivelson had finished telling what had happened aboard the *Javelin* when we joined the main crowd, and everybody was talking about what ought to be done with Steve Ravick. Oddly enough, the most bloodthirsty were the banker and the professor. Well, maybe it wasn’t so odd. They were smart enough to know what Steve Ravick was really doing to Port Sandor, and it hurt them as much as it did the hunters. Dad and Bish seemed to be the only ones present who weren’t in favor of going down to Hunters’ Hall right away and massacring everybody in it, and then doing the same at the Municipal Building.

===Chinese===
我们汇进人群时，乔·基维尔森已讲完标枪号上发生的一切，人人都在议论该拿史蒂夫·拉维克怎么办。说来奇怪，杀气最重的偏偏是那位银行家和教授。唉，或许也不算奇怪。他们够聪明，看得清史蒂夫·拉维克实际上在把桑多尔港糟蹋成什么样；这痛处扎在他们身上，不比扎在猎怪手们身上浅。在场的似乎只有爸爸和比什不赞成立刻杀到猎手会堂、把里头的人斩尽杀绝，然后照方抓药，再到市政大楼来上一遭。

===Original===
“That’s what I say!” Joe Kivelson was shouting. “Let’s go clean out both rats’ nests. Why, there must be a thousand hunter-ship men at the waterfront, and look how many people in town who want to help. We got enough men to eat Hunters’ Hall whole.”

“You’ll find it slightly inedible, Joe,” Bish told him. “Ravick has about thirty men of his own and fifteen to twenty city police. He has at least four 50 mm’s on the landing stage above, and he has half a dozen heavy machine guns and twice that many light 7 mm’s.”

===Chinese===
「我就是这个意思！」乔·基维尔森正吼着。「去把两个老鼠窝一锅端了。嘿，滨水区准有一千号猎怪船的汉子，城里想搭把手的人又有那么多。咱们的人手，足够把猎手会堂生吞下去。」

「你会发现它有点啃不动，乔，」比什对他说。「拉维克有三十来个自己的亲信，外加十五到二十名城市警察。上面顶层降落台至少架着四门 50 毫米炮，还有六挺重机枪，轻型 7 毫米机枪数目再翻一番。」

===Original===
“Bish is right,” somebody else said. “They have the vehicle port on the street level barricaded, and they have the two floors on the level below sealed off. We got men all around it and nobody can get out, but if we try to blast our way in, it’s going to cost us like Nifflheim.”

“You mean you’re just going to sit here and talk about it and not do anything?” Joe demanded.

“We’re going to do something, Joe,” Dad told him. “But we’ve got to talk about what we’re going to do, and how we’re going to do it, or it’ll be us who’ll get wiped out.”

===Chinese===
「比什说得对，」另一个人道。「他们把临街层的车辆门廊堵死了，下一层的两层楼面也封了个严实。我们的人四面包围着，谁也出不去；可要是硬往里轰，那代价就得尼弗尔海姆（Nifflheim）见了。」

「你是说，大家就坐在这儿空谈，什么也不干？」乔质问道。

「我们会干的，乔，」爸爸对他说。「但总得先商量清楚干什么、怎么干，不然被一锅端掉的就是我们。」

===Original===
“Well, we’ll have to decide on what it’ll be, pretty quick,” Mohandas Gandhi Feinberg said.

“What are things like at the Municipal Building?” Oscar Fujisawa asked. “You say Ravick has fifteen to twenty city cops at Hunters’ Hall. Where are the rest of them? That would only be five to ten.”

“At the Municipal Building,” Bish said. “Hallstock’s holed up there, trying to pretend that nothing out of the ordinary is happening.”

===Chinese===
「那行，可得快些定下章程才好，」莫汉达斯·甘地·范伯格（Mohandas Gandhi Feinberg）说。

「市政大楼那边情形如何？」奥斯卡·藤泽问。「你说拉维克在猎手会堂有十五到二十名城市警察，其余的人呢？满打满算也就还剩五到十个。」

「在市政大楼，」比什说。「霍尔斯特克龟缩在那儿，一心装作太平无事，没出任何非常之事。」

===Original===
“Good. Let’s go to the Municipal Building, first,” Oscar said. “Take a couple of hundred men, make a lot of noise, shoot out a few windows and all yell, ‘Hang Mort Hallstock!’ loud enough, and he’ll recall the cops he has at Hunters’ Hall to save his own neck. Then the rest of us can make a quick rush and take Hunters’ Hall.”

“We’ll have to keep our main force around Hunters’ Hall while we’re demonstrating at the Municipal Building,” Corkscrew Finnegan said. “We can’t take a chance on Ravick’s getting away.”

“I couldn’t care less whether he gets away or not,” Oscar said. “I don’t want Steve Ravick’s blood. I just want him out of the Cooperative, and if he runs out from it now, he’ll never get back in.”

===Chinese===
「好。那就先打市政大楼，」奥斯卡说。「带上两百来人，闹出大动静，打碎几扇窗，再齐声高喊『绞死莫特·霍尔斯特克（Mort Hallstock）！』，喊得够响，他为了保自个儿的脑袋，准会把守猎手会堂的警察调回去。然后我们其余的人猛扑上去，拿下猎手会堂。」

「咱们在市政大楼示威的当口，主力还得围住猎手会堂不放，」「螺丝锥」芬尼根说。「可不能赌运气，让拉维克跑了。」

「他跑不跑，与我何干，」奥斯卡说。「我不要史蒂夫·拉维克的血，只要他滚出合作社；他这会儿若是自己跑了，就再也别想回得来。」

===Original===
“You want him, and you want him alive,” Bish Ware said. “Ravick has close to four million sols banked on Terra. Every millisol of that’s money he’s stolen from the monster-hunters of this planet, through the Cooperative. If you just take him out and string him up, you’ll have the Nifflheim of a time getting hold of any of it.”

That made sense to all the ship captains, even Joe Kivelson, after Dad reminded him of how much the salvage job on the *Javelin* was going to cost. It took Sigurd Ngozori a couple of minutes to see the point, but then, hanging Steve Ravick wasn’t going to cost the Fidelity & Trust Company anything.

===Chinese===
「你要抓他，就得要活的，」比什·韦尔说。「拉维克在特拉存着将近四百万索尔的款子。那笔钱一个毫索尔一个毫索尔，全是他借着合作社、从这颗行星的猎怪手身上偷来的。你要是干脆把他拖出去吊死，再想追回其中一星半点，可就难得下尼弗尔海姆了。」

这话让全体船长都觉得在理，连乔·基维尔森也不例外——那还是爸爸提醒了他，打捞标枪号得花多少钱之后。西格德·恩戈佐里琢磨了两分钟才转过弯来；不过话说回来，绞死史蒂夫·拉维克，忠信信托公司（Fidelity & Trust Company）一个子儿也不用出。

===Original===
“Well, this isn’t my party,” Glenn Murell said, “but I’m too much of a businessman to see how watching somebody kick on the end of a rope is worth four million sols.”

“Four million sols,” Bish said, “and wondering, the rest of your lives, whether it was justice or just murder.”

The Buddhist priest looked at him, a trifle startled. After all, he was the only clergyman in the crowd; he ought to have thought of that, instead of this outrageous mock-bishop.

===Chinese===
「这个嘛，这场热闹不是我的戏码，」格伦·穆雷尔说，「可我到底是个生意人，实在看不出眼看什么人在绳套头上蹬腿，怎么就值四百万索尔。」

「四百万索尔，」比什说，「再加上后半辈子心里嘀咕：那到底是伸张正义，还是干脆就是谋杀。」

那位禅宗法师望着他，微微一怔。毕竟，满场只有他一位神职人员；这话本该由他先想到，而不是让这个离谱的「冒牌主教」（比什〔Bish〕恰是「主教」〔Bishop〕的谐音）抢了先。

===Original===
“I think it’s a good scheme,” Dad said. “Don’t mass any more men around Hunters’ Hall than necessary. You don’t want the police to be afraid to leave when Hallstock calls them in to help him at Municipal Building.”

Bish Ware rose. “I think I’ll see what I can do at Hunters’ Hall, in the meantime,” he said. “I’m going to see if there’s some way in from the First or Second Level Down. Walt, do you still have that sleep-gas gadget of yours?”

===Chinese===
「我看这计划不坏，」爸爸说。「猎手会堂四周，人手不必多聚。别弄得霍尔斯特克召他们回市政大楼救驾时，那帮警察吓得不敢挪窝。」

比什·韦尔站起身。「趁这工夫，我想去猎手会堂那边看看能使上什么劲，」他说。「我要去看看，从下第一层（First Level Down）或者下第二层，有没有路子能进去。沃尔特，你那件催眠气体的小玩意儿还带在身上吗？」

===Original===
I nodded. It was, ostensibly, nothing but an oversized pocket lighter, just the sort of a thing a gadget-happy kid would carry around. It worked perfectly as a lighter, too, till you pushed in on a little gismo on the side. Then, instead of producing a flame, it squirted out a small jet of sleep gas. It would knock out a man; it would almost knock out a Zarathustra *veldtbeest*. I’d bought it from a spaceman on the *Cape Canaveral*. I’d always suspected that he’d stolen it on Terra, because it was an expensive little piece of work, but was I going to ride a bicycle six hundred and fifty light-years to find out who it belonged to? One of the chemists’ shops at Port Sandor made me up some fills for it, and while I had never had to use it, it was a handy thing to have in some of the places I had to follow stories into, and it wouldn’t do anybody any permanent damage, the way a gun would.

===Chinese===
我点点头。明面上，它不过是只特大号的袖珍打火机，正是那种见了新鲜玩意儿就手痒的半大孩子会随身揣着的东西。它当打火机用也着实好使，直到你把侧面一个小机关按进去，那时它喷出的就不是火苗，而是一小股催眠气体。放倒一个大人不在话下；放倒一头查拉图斯特拉（Zarathustra）草原兽（*veldtbeest*），也只差一点火候。这是我从卡纳维拉尔角号（*Cape Canaveral*）上一个太空人手里买来的。我一直疑心那是他在特拉偷的赃——这么件小东西，做工可着实不便宜——可难道要我蹬着自行车跑上六百五十光年，去查清它原是谁家的吗？桑多尔港有家药房替我配了几管补充气，虽说我从没用上过，但在我追新闻不得不钻的一些去处，带着它总归是件趁手的东西，而且不像枪那样，会给人留下好不了的伤。

===Original===
“Yes; it’s down in my room. I’ll get it for you,” I said.

“Be careful, Bish,” Dad said. “That gang would kill you sooner than look at you.”

“Who, me?” Bish staggered into a table and caught hold of it. “Who’d wanna hurt me? I’m just good ol’ Bish Ware. *Good ol’ Bish!* nobody hurt him; he’sh everybody’s friend.” He let go of the table and staggered into a chair, upsetting it. Then he began to sing:

===Chinese===
「在；就搁我楼下房里。我去给你取，」我说。

「当心点，比什，」爸爸说。「那帮家伙杀你比看你一眼还快。」

「谁，我吗？」比什一个趔趄撞上桌子，一把扶住。「谁会想来伤我呀？我就是老好人比什·韦尔呀。*老好比什！*没人伤他的；他呀，谁谁都是他的朋友，嗝。」他撒开桌子，又一个趔趄跌进椅子，把椅子带翻了。然后他唱了起来：

===Original===
> “Come all ye hardy spacemen, and harken while I tell
> Of fluorine-tainted Nifflheim, the Planetary Hell.”

===Chinese===
> 「来吧，剽悍的太空人全体，且听我唱分明：
> 氟毒弥漫的尼弗尔海姆，那座行星地狱之城。」

===Original===
Involuntarily, I began clapping my hands. It was a superb piece of acting—Bish Ware sober playing Bish Ware drunk, and that’s not an easy role for anybody to play. Then he picked up the chair and sat down on it.

“Who do you have around Hunters’ Hall, and how do I get past them?” he asked. “I don’t want a clipful from somebody on my own side.”

===Chinese===
我不由自主地鼓起掌来。这是一场一流的表演——清醒的比什·韦尔扮演烂醉的比什·韦尔，这个角色搁谁身上都不好演。随后他扶起椅子，坐了上去。

「猎手会堂四周，你们布了哪些人，我怎么过去才不吃枪子儿？」他问。「我可不想挨自己人一梭子。」

===Original===
Nip Spazoni got a pencil and a pad of paper and began drawing a plan.

“This is Second Level Down,” he said. “We have a car here, with a couple of men in it. It’s watching this approach here. And we have a ship’s boat, over here, with three men in it, and a 7 mm machine gun. And another car—no, a jeep, here. Now, up on the First Level Down, we have two ships’ boats, one here, and one here. The password is ‘Exotic,’ and the countersign is ‘Organics.’” He grinned at Murell. “Compliment to your company.”

===Chinese===
尼普·斯帕佐尼（Nip Spazoni）拿了支铅笔、一本便笺簿，动手画起草图来。

「这是下第二层，」他说。「这儿我们有一辆车，车里两个人，盯着这条进路。这边还有一条舰载小艇，艇上三个人、一挺 7 毫米机枪。另有一辆车——不对，一辆吉普，在这儿。再往上，下第一层，我们有两条小艇，一条在这儿，一条在这儿。口令是『珍奇』（Exotic），回令是『有机物』（Organics）。」他冲穆雷尔咧嘴一笑。「向贵公司致敬。」

===Original===
“Good enough. I’ll want a bottle of liquor. My breath needs a little touching up, and I may want to offer somebody a drink. If I could get inside that place, there’s no telling what I might be able to do. If one man can get in and put a couple of guards to sleep, an army can get in after him.”

===Chinese===
「好极了。我还要一瓶酒。我这口里的酒气还得添一添，兴许还得请谁喝上一杯。我要是能进到那地方里头，天知道我能做成什么。一个人进得去、用催眠气体放倒两个哨兵，一支大军就能跟着进去。」

===Original===
*Brother*, I thought, *if he pulls this one off, he’s in.* Nobody around Port Sandor will ever look down on Bish Ware again, not even Joe Kivelson. I began thinking about the detective agency idea again, and wondered if he’d want a junior partner. Ware & Boyd, Planetwide Detective Agency.

I went down to the floor below with him and got him my lighter gas-projector and a couple of spare fills for it, and found the bottle of Baldur honey-rum that Dad had been sure was around somewhere. I was kind of doubtful about that, and he noticed my hesitation in giving it to him and laughed.

===Chinese===
*老兄*，我心想，*这要让他办成了，他可就彻底翻身了。*桑多尔港再没有人会看不起比什·韦尔，连乔·基维尔森也不会。我又开始琢磨起那桩私家侦探社的主意来，寻思他要不要一位初级合伙人。韦尔-博伊德全行星侦探社（Ware & Boyd, Planetwide Detective Agency）。

我随他下到楼下一层，取来我那支打火机形的气体喷射器给他，又配了两管备用填充气，还找出了那瓶爸爸咬定就藏在什么地方的巴尔德蜂蜜朗姆酒（Baldur honey-rum）。这件事我心里多少犯嘀咕，他看出我递酒时的迟疑，笑了。

===Original===
“Don’t worry, Walt,” he said. “This is strictly for protective coloration—and odoration. I shall be quite sparing with it, I assure you.”

I shook hands with him, trying not to be too solemn about it, and he went down in the elevator and I went up the stairs to the floor above. By this time, the Port Sandor Vigilance Committee had gotten itself sorted out. The rank-and-file Vigilantes were standing around yacking at one another, and a smaller group—Dad and Sigurd Ngozori and the Reverend Sugitsuma and Oscar and Joe and Corkscrew and Nip and the Mahatma—were in a huddle around Dad’s editorial table, discussing strategy and tactics.

===Chinese===
「放心，沃尔特，」他说。「这纯粹是充当保护色——外加保护气味。我保证，用起来会十分节制。」

我同他握了握手，尽量不显得太郑重；他乘电梯下去了，我顺楼梯上了楼上一层。到这时，桑多尔港自警委员会（Port Sandor Vigilance Committee）已经把自己的班底理出了头绪。自警队的普通队员四下里站着闲扯，另一小拨人——爸爸、西格德·恩戈佐里、杉妻法师（the Reverend Sugitsuma）、奥斯卡、乔、「螺丝锥」、尼普和「圣雄」——围着爸爸的编辑桌抱成一团，讨论起战略战术来。

===Original===
“Well, we’d better get back to the docks before it starts,” Corkscrew was saying. “No hunter crew will follow anybody but their own ships’ officers.”

“We’ll have to have somebody the uptown people will follow,” Oscar said. “These people won’t take orders from a woolly-pants hunter captain. How about you, Sigurd?”

The banker shook his head. “Ralph Boyd’s the man for that,” he said.

===Chinese===
「这个嘛，动手之前，我们最好赶回船坞去，」「螺丝锥」正说着。「猎怪船的船员，除了自己船上的船长船副，谁的话也不听。」

「上城的人，也得有个肯跟随的头面人物，」奥斯卡说。「这些人可不会听一个穿毛裤的猎怪船船长号令。你来怎么样，西格德？」

银行家摇头。「干这个，拉尔夫·博伊德（Ralph Boyd）才是那块料，」他说。

===Original===
“Ralph’s needed right here; this is G.H.Q.,” Oscar said. “This is a job that’s going to have to be run from one central command. We’ve got to make sure the demonstration against Hallstock and the operation against Hunters’ Hall are synchronized.”

“I have about a hundred and fifty workmen, and they all have or can get something to shoot with,” another man said. I looked around, and saw that it was Casmir Oughourlian, of Rodriguez & Oughourlian Shipyards. “They’ll follow me, but I’m not too well known uptown.”

===Chinese===
「拉尔夫在这儿脱不开身；这儿是总指挥部（G.H.Q.），」奥斯卡说。「这一仗，必须从一个统一的指挥中枢来调度。我们得确保对霍尔斯特克的示威行动和进攻猎手会堂的行动步调一致。」

「我手头有一百五十来个工人，个个手里有枪，或者弄得到枪，」另一个声音说。我环顾一圈，看见说话的是罗德里格斯-乌古尔良造船厂（Rodriguez & Oughourlian Shipyards）的卡斯米尔·乌古尔良（Casmir Oughourlian）。「他们肯跟我走，只是我在上城名气不够响。」

===Original===
“Hey, Professor Hartzenbosch,” Mohandas Feinberg said. “You’re a respectable-looking duck; you ever have any experience leading a lynch mob?”

Everybody laughed. So, to his credit, did the professor.

“I’ve had a lot of experience with children,” the professor said. “Children are all savages. So are lynch mobs. Things that are equal to the same thing are equal to one another. Yes, I’d say so.”

===Chinese===
「嘿，哈岑博施教授，」莫汉达斯·范伯格说。「您老这副模样，一看就是体面人物；带领私刑队（lynch mob），可有过经验？」

众人哄笑。教授也笑了——这一点得给他记上一功。

「带孩子嘛，我的经验可多着呢，」教授说。「孩子全是野人。私刑队也一样。与同一事物相等的事物，彼此也相等。嗯，我看带得成。」

===Original===
“All right,” Dad said. “Say I’m Chief of Staff, or something. Oscar, you and Joe and Corkscrew and the rest of you decide who’s going to take overall command of the hunters. Casmir, you’ll command your workmen, and anybody else from the shipyards and engine works and repair shops and so on. Sigurd, you and the Reverend, here, and Professor Hartzenbosch gather up all the uptown people you can. Now, we’ll have to decide on how much force we need to scare Mort Hallstock, and how we’re going to place the main force that will attack Hunters’ Hall.”

“I think we ought to wait till we see what Bish Ware can do,” Oscar said. “Get our gangs together, and find out where we’re going to put who, but hold off the attack for a while. If he can get inside Hunters’ Hall, we may not even need this demonstration at the Municipal Building.”

===Chinese===
「好，」爸爸说。「就算我当参谋长（Chief of Staff）之类的角色吧。奥斯卡，你同乔、『螺丝锥』还有你们几位商量定，由谁出任全体猎怪手的总指挥。卡斯米尔，你指挥你的工人，外加造船厂、机器厂、修理厂等等来的一切人手。西格德，你同这位法师、还有哈岑博施教授，把上城能拉到的人尽量聚拢来。接下来要定的是：吓住莫特·霍尔斯特克需要多少人马，进攻猎手会堂的主力又该怎么摆布。」

「我看，该等一等，瞧瞧比什·韦尔能使出什么手段，」奥斯卡说。「各路人马先聚齐，各自的位置先排定，进攻却暂缓一缓。他要真能进到猎手会堂里头，市政大楼这场示威，说不定都省了。」

===Original===
Joe Kivelson started to say something. The rest of his fellow ship captains looked at him severely, and he shut up. Dad kept on jotting down figures of men and 50 mm guns and vehicles and auto weapons we had available.

He was still doing it when the fire alarm started.

===Chinese===
乔·基维尔森刚要开口，其余几位同僚船长朝他狠狠一瞪，他便住了嘴。爸爸继续往下列数目：可用的人手、50 毫米炮、车辆和自动武器，一样样记下数字。

他还在写着，火警响了起来。
